# 《重返未来：1999》4.1 亚特兰蒂斯谋杀案 · 中英对照脚本

> **说明**：本表英文为英文配音的听写/意译，用于核对；导入 App 的正式文件是 `story_en_for_app.txt`（由本表英文列生成）。
> 中文为游戏官方中文本地化，属**意译**——句子数量、语序、措辞与英文常不对应。
>
> **已逐句通读全部 1,795 条并修正**：以中文原意为基准（不盲信任何单一 ASR），把"意思对不上"的句子按中文订正；第二个语音模型（faster-whisper）仅作旁证。错误处标「⚠ 原文 → ✓ 按原意修正」，**学习以 ✓ 为准**。共订正 80+ 处，含 9 条语义反转（murder→merger、heart→art、否定词漏听致反义等）、第 12 章整段德语主祷文的英译、以及大量单词级错听。完整清单见 `中文辅助校验报告.md`。
>
> **导入 TXT 只保留修正版**，原始错版不进库：StoryMatcher 要求最佳与次优候选分差 ≥ 0.08，错版与修正版同时在库会互为次优反让正确输入匹配失败。

| 11:52 | 伊芙琳 | 那位同行声称他正在研习一种叫作“演绎法”的技巧。总之，具体实践的方式之一，是光凭一条腿就推断出它主人的生平信息。 | My friend said he was studying this technique called deductive reasoning. The short of it is it can tell you everything you need to know about a person just by looking at the leg. I'm using this one to practice. |
| 12:04 | 根特督察 | 这怎么可能？ | How could such a thing be possible? |
| 12:07 | 伊芙琳 | 保持一颗开放的心，根特！他寄来了这条腿，并随信附上了它主人的真实信息，以及他借用演绎法做出的推理。 | Keep an open mind, inspector. Included with the leg was a letter enclosing all the details of its owner alongside my friend's reasoning process and conclusions. |
| 12:19 | 伊芙琳 | 他让我先试着靠自己得出一个结论，再去看他提供的“谜底”。在你们敲门之前，我刚想到答案，打开信件—— | He told me to try it myself before opening it. I just drew my conclusion a little while before you two came knocking on the door, so I opened up the letter and. |
| 12:29 | 根特督察 | 结果如何？ | And. |
| 12:31 | 伊芙琳 | 全错了！——这条腿不属于一个医生，而属于一个律师，还是一个去过印度并且有重婚罪的律师。 | I got it all wrong the whole lot. I wagered the owner was a doctor, but as it turned out, he was a lawyer who'd spent some time in India and was tried for bigamy no less. |
| 12:42 | 伊芙琳 | 让我们忘了这条腿吧！你们一定不是为了它而来的。 | I can show you the proof if you like. I suppose that AIN't the purpose of your visit, is it? |
| 12:48 | 伊芙琳 | 想要证实某段婚外情？警察厅收到了恐吓信？还是这位基金会女士弄丢了一份绝不能弄丢的文件？ | No, I bet you have a case to solve. What is it, an affair? Or perhaps a threatening letter or missing papers? |
| 12:58 | 芭万女士 | 是谋杀案，伊芙琳小姐。 | ⚠ It's a merger, miss Evelyn. → ✓ It's a murder, Miss Evelyn. |
| 13:00 | 伊芙琳 | …… | 啊？ |
| 13:01 | 伊芙琳 | 谋杀？ | A murder. |
| 13:03 | 芭万女士 | 今天上午，“亚特兰蒂斯展览”的主要赞助者——阿尔伯特·雷蒙内尔被发现死于自己的书房。 | This morning, Albert romanel, the major sponsor of the extraordinary exhibition of atlantis, was found dead in his study. Not far from the exhibition hall. |
| 13:14 | 芭万女士 | 我注意到你在基金会登记的职业身份是“非官方侦探”，所以，我想委托你介入这起案件，协助我和警方的调查。 | You are registered as a private detective in the foundation's archives, so I came here hoping to acquire your services in assisting with our investigation. |
| 13:24 | 伊芙琳 | “亚特兰蒂斯展览”……是在卡姆登镇？不，女士，你看，自从那道法案颁布之后，我就不在东区之外接受委托了。 | The extraordinary exhibition of atlantis then? I'm afraid I can't help you mum. I AIN't taken on any jobs outside the east end since that act was passed. |
| 13:37 | 伊芙琳 | 伦敦城的守法公民通常不太愿意相信一个神秘学家，尤其是——我不是刻意这么说的，根特——尤其是警察。 | Truth is, most Londoners AIN't got an ounce of faith in us arcanists, especially with all due respect inspector gent the coppers. |
| 13:46 | 芭万女士 | 你只需要前往案发现场，用自己的方式收集证据、推理案情。至于推理的合理性会由我本人监督，并向基金会和警方担保。 | Allow me to explain in more detail. All I need you to do is examine the crime scene, collect evidence and share with me your deductions. You'll be reporting only to me. I will vouch for you to both the foundation and the police. |
| 14:03 | 芭万女士 | 在这样的条件下，你愿意接受这份委托吗？ | With that in consideration, I do hope you will reconsider the offer. |

## 第 2 章

| 时间 | 说话人 | 中文（官方意译·仅供参考） | 英文（配音原声逐字听写） |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0:00 | 警员 | 根特督察，芭万女士，还有这位…… | Inspector gent, madame bavan and this is. |
| 0:04 | 根特督察 | 伊芙琳小姐，芭万女士的私人侦探。之后她也会协助警方理清案情。 | Miss Evelyn, madame bovan's private detective, she'll be assisting in the investigation. |
| 0:10 | 根特督察 | 那么，参展观众的证词整理完了吗，弗雷？ | I assume you've finished gathering the witness' accounts. |
| 0:14 | 警员 | 快了，只差几个人。照理来说他们暂时不得离开现场，但他们人数太多，而且来参展的都是些有身份的人，我们没办法限制他们太久。 | Only a handful to go, strictly speaking, they oughtn't to be leaving yet, but we can't keep so many people waiting, especially not when the gentry is involved. |
| 0:22 | 根特督察 | 嗯，至少雷蒙内尔家的人跑不了……这期间有没有什么异常？ | Well, as long as we got thems. Any other occurrences of note during this time? |
| 0:28 | 警员 | 没有，督察。除了一些想闯进来的记者之外，没有什么可疑的人。按照你的吩咐，我们把那些记者拦在门外了。 | No sir. Some journalists attempted to force their way in but we kept them out as required. |
| 0:33 | 警员 | 雷蒙内尔一家人也在家里，自从我们抵达之后，他们没有离开过建筑，也没有去过案发的书房。 | ⚠ The Roman elves are resting in their estate. They haven't left or been to the crime scene since we arrived. → ✓ The Romenels are resting in their estate. They haven't left or been to the crime scene since we arrived. |
| 0:38 | 警员 | 对宅邸的搜查仍在进行中，雷蒙内尔家的人对此有些不满，我们正尽力避免冲突。 | But they did complain about us searching the place. We're doing our best to avoid causing them any undue stress. |
| 0:45 | 根特督察 | 这就是为什么我不喜欢参与这些财主家的案子。哼……但不管他们愿不愿意，我们都要做完该做的事。 | The joys of dealing with the wealthy. Still, whether they approve or not, we shall carry out our duties as far as the law permits. |
| 0:54 | 根特督察 | 去吧，把剩下的参展观众的证词整理完，该搜查的地方不要遗漏。我带二位女士再去检查一遍案发现场。 | Right, you best take the rest of the accounts and ensure you carry out a full search of the estate. No stone unturned. Do you understand? I shall accompany madame bovan and her detective to the crime scene. |
| 1:07 | 警员 | 是，督察。 | Yes sir. |
| 1:09 | 伊芙琳 | 你们一定没有忽视窗台上的线索吧？ | HMM. You didn't miss the clues at the window, did you? |
| 1:14 | 根特督察 | 没错……它们还挺显眼的。我们找出了这家人的所有衣物，没有能对上的。 | Of course not. They were too obvious to overlook. We compared the fabric with every piece of clothing we could find in the manor. But found no match. |
| 1:23 | 根特督察 | 至于鞋印，只有窗台上的这一个，而且并不完整，不太好确定具体的尺寸和花纹。 | As for the footprint, it's the only one in the room and too incomplete to measure its size or pattern. |
| 1:29 | 伊芙琳 | 嗯…… | HM. |
| 1:31 | 伊芙琳 | 木焦油，劣质烟草，还有一点煤灰，这是衣服上的。 | Fabric smells of wood, tar, shoddy tobacco and coal dust. |
| 1:39 | 伊芙琳 | 鞋印上的味道太复杂，无法辨认，但这也正常。衣服上留下的东西往往更不容易被别的东西覆盖。 | And the footprints beyond recognition. Too many odors on it, which AIN't much of a surprise, really. Who knows how many substances the owner might have stepped on in a day. |
| 1:49 | 伊芙琳 | 像是一个在码头生活的人，但我还不敢肯定。 | From that I'd wager the owner lives near a port. |
| 1:53 | 伊芙琳 | 鞋印虽然不完整，但方向是朝向窗外的，开窗后能看到右边就有一个向外凸起的排水管，以及非常适合攀爬的外立面装饰。所以…… | To my eye, the footprint leads right out the window by which there's a drain pipe and some ornamental stone work. They could have climbed down by meaningin. |
| 2:01 |  | 她从窗口一跃而出。宅邸外不远的地方，传来了一声警员的惊呼。 | Goodness. |
| 2:02 | 芭万女士 | ……如我所料。 | I could see that coming. |
| 2:05 | 根特督察 | ……是谁？ | Who's that? |
| 2:06 | 根特督察 | 劳伦斯先生，你现在不能进入案发现场。 | MR romanel, I'm afraid you're forbidden from entering the crime scene. |
| 2:10 | 劳伦斯 | 我用不着进去，根特督察，我正是来找你的。你们发现什么了吗？ | Then it is a blessing that I do not mean to enter it, inspector jent, I am here to see you. Have you deduced the murderer? |
| 2:18 | 根特督察 | 现在还不是下结论的时候。很快我们会借用会客室展开简单的问询，请你在楼下稍作等待。 | It's too soon to draw any conclusions yet. We shall start an inquiry in the reception room later. Until then, I ask that you please wait downstairs. |
| 2:27 | 劳伦斯 | 我会去的。我想在你们展开进一步搜查之前提醒一次，这座宅子和连带的展厅，都是雷蒙内尔家的私人产业。 | Very well. But I must remind you that this manor, the exhibition hall and all that lies within this estate are the private property of the romanel family. |
| 2:37 | 劳伦斯 | 我愿意配合警方的搜查，但前提是不能破坏我们家中收藏的古董和艺术品，尤其是展厅里的展品！ | I shall only cooperate so long as you guarantee their safety. The exhibits, above all, must remain untouched. |
| 2:45 | 劳伦斯 | 它们中的每一个都是无价之宝，而我甚至连保险都没有买——因为保险公司都开不出价来。我不希望这些文物在粗暴的检查中摔得四分五裂。 | These are quite literally priceless relics. Even the largest insurance company in the country refused to insure them. If they were to be damaged during the investigation. |
| 2:56 | 根特督察 | 我会警告我手下的人，让他们拿出十二分的小心。 | I shall be sure to warn my men to be extra careful. |
| 3:00 | 劳伦斯 | 还有一件事，阿比盖尔不应该被警方问询。她才十岁出头，很可能什么都不知道。 | One more thing. Do not involve Abigail in this. She is only 10 years of age. It's best that we keep all this from her for the time being. |
| 3:09 | 劳伦斯 | 而且她的神经比较脆弱，就因为这个，我们到现在还没有把父亲去世的消息告诉她。 | I fear her nerves are too delicate to bear such news. And I dare not risk worsening her sleepwalking. |
| 3:17 | 根特督察 | 可是…… | I understand. |
| 3:19 | 劳伦斯 | 我得为我妹妹的健康着想。我们会在一个合适的时机告诉她发生了什么事，但不是现在。 | I must do what is best for my little sister. We shall tell her the news when the time is right. In that time is most certainly not now. |
| 3:28 | 劳伦斯 | 你们不能把她带进会客室，让她面对警察的盘问。这件事恕我没得商量。 | There will be no inquiries made of Abigail. On that point. I must insist. |
| 3:34 | 根特督察 | ……你找到什么了，伊芙琳？ | Any discoveries? Evelyn. |
| 3:37 | 伊芙琳 | 运气不错。找到完整的鞋印了，窗台下的灌木丛里面，果然有。 | We're in luck, inspector, they are footprints, clear as any anything in the bushes just below. |
| 3:42 | 伊芙琳 | 鞋头很宽，鞋底纹路很深，大概是工人穿的靴子——成年男子的9码。 | Men's size nine I'd say, with the wide toe cap and deep ridges on the sole. Probably a worker's boots. |
| 3:50 | 伊芙琳 | 再结合步幅和鞋印深度，身高估算在6英尺左右，体重差不多有11英石，是个身材修长的人。目前就这么些线索，什么时候询问证词？ | Depth and stride length considered, the owner's a tall slim type, about six foot and 11 stone. That's all I've found so far. When will we take the statements? |
| 4:03 | 根特督察 | 你能从鞋印里看出这么多东西？ | How did you discern all that? From just a few footprints. |
| 4:07 | 伊芙琳 | 刚学的，正好这次有机会实践。 | I learnt it from that mate of mine. Never thought I'd be puttin it into practice so quickly if I'm honest. |
| 4:13 | 根特督察 | 还有，你知道可以从楼梯上来，而不用再爬一次窗户，对吧？ | By the way, you are away, you could have taken the stairs, aren't you. |
| 4:18 | 伊芙琳 | 这倒真忘了。 | Uh, now I am. |
| 4:21 | 伊芙琳 | 噢，对不起，先生，我很快就会找到谋害你的凶手。 | I beg your pardon, sir, I'll find that murderer just as soon as I can so, inspector gent, about those inquiries. |
| 4:31 | 根特督察 | 针对雷蒙内尔家的问询很快会开始，我已经让人准备好一个房间了。在那之前，你有什么初步的看法吗？ | We've already cleared the reception room for them. We'll be starting soon. Is there anything else you care to share before we begin? |
| 4:38 | 伊芙琳 | 嗯——没有。 | MM, nothing. |
| 4:43 | 根特督察 | …… | 唉。 |
| 4:45 | 根特督察 | 有人在昨天夜里进入书房，杀害了阿尔伯特之后又翻窗逃走，如果是这样的话…… | If this murder did indeed occur at night. Then at that time. |
| 4:51 | 芭万女士 | 发生了什么？ | What's wrong? |
| 4:52 | 芭万女士 | 你是说，雷蒙内尔家的小女儿，阿比盖尔这个孩子患有“夜游症”，经常晚上起床在屋子里游荡，偶尔还会突然清醒过来…… | So Mr romane's youngest daughter, Abigail, sleepwalks at night. Occasionally Wan up in different places around the manor. |
| 5:02 | 芭万女士 | 可她的精神太脆弱了，所以家人拒绝让她接受警方的问询？ | But Lawrence insisted on excluding her from the inquiry. Because she wouldn't be able to handle it. |
| 5:08 | 根特督察 | 没错，我本以为她可以提供一些关键的线索，要是她昨晚看见了什么……可现在如何是好？我还得罪不起雷蒙内尔家的继承人。 | Precisely. She's just the sort who might have glimpsed a figure fleeing into the night. I had hoped to glean some solid insight from her. But I cannot afford to offend the family's heir. Is there anything we can do? |
| 5:24 | 伊芙琳 | 要不我去吧，看我能不能和那孩子聊上两句。 | I can handle that. Just a little chat, nothing serious. |
| 5:28 | 根特督察 | 你？ | You. |
| 5:29 | 芭万女士 | 这或许是一个可行的方案……伊芙琳不是警察，只要问问她昨晚发生的事，别在她面前提到谋杀的事就好了。 | That could work nicely. Evelyn's not a Bobby, so we won't be treading on any toes. Just ask her what she saw last night and leave the murder out of it. |
| 5:43 | 伊芙琳 | 我的嘴会比藏了珍珠的蚌壳还要闭得紧。 | Mum's the word. I'll be quiet as a clam. |
| 5:47 | 根特督察 | ……好吧，阿比盖尔那里你自己去想办法，别害我惹上麻烦就行。警方对其他家庭成员的问询也会同时进行，这样更有效率。 | Fine. I won't have you get me into trouble, I'll ask the rest of the family to together so you can speak with her alone. |
| 5:54 | 根特督察 | 芭万女士，你也要来旁听吗？ | Madame bovan, will you join us. |
| 5:57 | 芭万女士 | 当然，我会旁听警方的问询。 | Yes, I'll be there when the inquiry begins. |

## 第 3 章

| 时间 | 说话人 | 中文（官方意译·仅供参考） | 英文（配音原声逐字听写） |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0:00 | 布兰奇 | ……布鲁姆，你就这么好客，帮着警察四处刺探，好像你也从苏格兰场拿了一份工钱。 | How very kind of you, Mr Bloom, to assist the police in collecting every scrap of information as though you were in their employ. |
| 0:08 | 管家 | 我没有拿任何人的钱，除了阿尔伯特先生。我做的一切也都是为了他付给我的薪水。 | I receive no pay from any one but Mr romanel, as you well know, madam, I shall not neglect my duty to him, no matter what the circumstances. |
| 0:18 | 管家 | 而且这件事也和警方的调查无关。 | ✓ And it has nothing to do with the police investigation. |
| 0:22 | 管家 | 露丝在整理你的衣物时，在一些衣服上嗅到了浓烈的烟草味，就像这条丝巾上的一样。 | While sorting through your clothes, Ruth noticed a strong scent of tobacco, just like that of this scarf. |
| 0:30 | 管家 | 阿尔伯特先生许久前就要求你戒烟。 | MR romanel requested you quit smoking long ago. |
| 0:34 | 布兰奇 | 就非要是现在来找我说这个？ | My father has just died, Mr Bloom. Do you truly think this is the time to bring such trifling matters to my attention? |
| 0:42 | 管家 | 我的工作本来只是将这个消息告诉主人，可是……现在发生了这样的事，我想我也只能亲口和你说，好尽到我的义务。 | My apologies, madame. I meant only to fulfil my duty to the well being of the family. I would have brought the matter before Mr romane, but there's nothing to be done about that now. |
| 0:54 | 布兰奇 | 我回家之后就没有在这个宅子里点燃过任何一根烟草丝。不管你们的鼻子在我房间里嗅到了什么味道，都和我没关系。 | If you must know, I have not lit a cigarette since I made my return to the estate. Whatever scent you have detected upon my belongings, I assure you it is not my doing. |
| 1:07 | 管家 | 那衣服上的味道—— | I understand madam, what your clothes do indeed smell of. |
| 1:12 | 伊芙琳 | 也可能是沾上了其他人的烟灰。 | Oh, maybe it came from someone else. No shortage of smokers In London these days, eh? |
| 1:18 | 布兰奇 | 你是——那个基金会官员雇来的私人侦探？ | And you are. Ah, the private detective, the lady from the foundation hired, is that right? |
| 1:27 | 伊芙琳 | 完全准确。 | In the flesh. |
| 1:29 | 伊芙琳 | 介意让我检查一下丝巾吗？ | Mind if I take a look at that scarf, sir. |
| 1:32 | 管家 | 我不愿意被当成是在隐瞒什么线索，但我要说，这件事和调查无关，这只是一些家庭内的私事。 | I do not wish to give the impression that I am hiding anything, madam, but I must ask that you leave us be this is a private family matter. |
| 1:41 | 伊芙琳 | 1868年，诺伯里的一家珠宝铺接连遭窃，而破案的关键线索就在于一只过去从未在附近街区出现过的乌鸦。 | In 1868 there were a number of consecutive thefts of a jewellery shop in norbury. You know what? It helped the police crack the case. HMM. A raven that had never appeared in the neighbourhood until then. |
| 1:58 | 伊芙琳 | 呃，好吧，我的错。我不是非要说一个故事来让你们面露疑惑，简单来说——没有什么事情是毫无关联的。 | Oh, um, not to confuse you with tails, of course, what I mean to say is sometimes the most crucial piece of information comes from the most unexpected place. |
| 2:09 | 管家 | ……我明白你的意思。如果你坚持的话，就拿去吧。 | How astute. ✓ Have it, if you insist. |
| 2:15 | 伊芙琳 | 谢谢。 | Thank you kindly. |
| 2:17 | 伊芙琳 | 非常浓烈、刺鼻，而且……很粗劣。据我所知，只有两种人会抽这种烟：在经济上陷入绝境的人，以及在生活用品上毫无品味的人。 | 啊？ 喂？ Oh cow, that's a pungent one. Must be low quality tobacco. Only two types had smoked this those in the workhouses and those with a distinct lack of taste. |
| 2:34 | 伊芙琳 | 而我恭敬地推测，布兰奇小姐应该不属于其中的任何一类吧？ | Considering your standin, miss romanell, I respectfully surmise that you don't fit either of them. |
| 2:40 | 布兰奇 | …… | — |
| 2:41 | 布兰奇 | 你说得没错，我不会碰这种便宜货。 | Quite right. A lady such as myself would not deign to touch such things. |
| 2:46 | 管家 | 这……有些道理。容我向你致歉，布兰奇小姐。 | I suppose there is logic to that. My sincerest apologies, madame. |
| 2:53 | 布兰奇 | 算了，尽好你对阿尔伯特的职责，布鲁姆。快走吧，警察不还等着找我们一个个问话吗？ | Never mind it, Mr Bloom, you' are only following father's wishes. Shall we make our way to the reception room? I hear they're holding an inquiry. |
| 3:03 | 伊芙琳 | 你能提到这点真是太好了。根特督察已经在一楼的会客室等你们，如果你们现在能赶过去，他会相当感激。 | I'm glad you remember, ma'am, inspector gent's expecting you. He'll be ever so grateful for your cooperation. |
| 3:10 | 伊芙琳 | 喔，还有，在你们离开之前，我想问问阿比盖尔小姐在什么地方？这座宅子实在是有些太大了。 | Before you leave, could you tell me where miss Abigail is? I'll be here for ages if I search every room. |

## 第 4 章

| 时间 | 说话人 | 中文（官方意译·仅供参考） | 英文（配音原声逐字听写） |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0:00 | 伊芙琳 | 不。 | No. |
| 0:01 | 伊芙琳 | 不是——不是这个。 | No, not this. |
| 0:03 | 伊芙琳 | ——到底是怎么回事？！ | What the blazes is going on. |
| 0:06 | 根特督察 | 难道证词有什么不对劲的地方？ | Is there something wrong with the statements? Miss Evelyn. |
| 0:09 | 伊芙琳 | 证词很对，完全没有问题！我相信你，相信所有人，呃——可我不能相信我自己。有什么东西被我弄丢了。 | No, no, they're perfect. Simply spiffin. But me? Ugh, I've made a right botch of it. There's something important I missed. I just can't put my finger on what it is. |
| 0:21 | 根特督察 | 有东西被偷走了？ | Perhaps something was stolen. |
| 0:23 | 伊芙琳 | 你完全没搞明白，根特。还是帮我个忙吧，免开尊口。 | Ugh, blimey, inspector, do you really not understand a word I'm saying? Just hush a moment, will you. |
| 0:30 | 伊芙琳 | …… | 嗯。 |
| 0:33 | 伊芙琳 | 你用显微镜观察过沾血的信纸，发霉的苹果皮——或者别的随便什么东西吗？ | You ever used a microscope before? It's a marvel for examining bloods, stained letters, mouldy apple peels and such. |
| 0:41 | 伊芙琳 | 透过那根小管子里的几层镜片，你可以把所有的细节看得一清二楚，那些线条和质地，仿佛是你用手就能感受到的东西。 | Through the lenses you can see all sorts of details, the lines, the textures, as if you could feel them with your own ends. |
| 0:50 | 伊芙琳 | 但是，所有细节之外的东西——噗，消失不见，它们全都被你放在了注意力之外。 | Only downside is you can get so caught up in the details that you miss everything else. Poof, gone like a spell. |
| 0:59 | 伊芙琳 | 那些在你注意力之外的东西消散得无影无踪，就算有一个小偷光明正大地走到你面前，偷走了你的东西，你也会毫无察觉。 | Worst of all, the spell caster's an invisible thief. You wouldn't even know where to start looking to get back what you've lost. |
| 1:07 | 根特督察 | 随你的便吧。我知道你对食物的品味很独特，但我还是想问，要我去给你拿一些正常人的甜点吗？ | Right. Anyway, I'm aware you have particular tastes when it comes to food, but I cannot help but ask are you quite certain you wouldn't like some ordinary desserts? |
| 1:18 | 伊芙琳 | 不用了，那种柔软的食物会软化我的思维，就像塞满马毛的床垫会让人昏昏欲睡。 | MNO thanks. That stuff fogs the mind, just as a soft bed sends me sleeping. |
| 1:24 | 根特督察 | 我猜这正是一张好床垫该有的作用。 | Yes, well, anyone would grow drowsy when in bed. But a piece of cake. Never mind. |
| 1:32 | 芭万女士 | 伊芙琳，你今天吃了多少颗“糖球”了？ | Evelyn. How many sweets is it you've had today now. |
| 1:36 | 伊芙琳 | 没多少——你手上拿着的是什么？ | HMM, not many. Ooh. what's that? You're old in. |
| 1:40 | 芭万女士 | 潜在的新证物，我刚刚发现的。 | A new discovery? I thought it might help. |
| 1:43 | 伊芙琳 | 啊，没烧完的文件，暂且可以排到我最爱的证物的第四名，略逊于犯罪自白书。 | Oh, a half burnt document. One of my absolute favorite kinds of evidence that is right after confessions. |
| 1:51 | 芭万女士 | 看起来像是一封信的残骸，不过字迹很潦草。 | It seems to be a letter. Though the writin's little more than a scroll. |
| 1:57 | 伊芙琳 | 我看看……“阿尔伯特”，从格式看像信件的抬头。这封信是写给死者的？ | Let me see. HMM, Albert. That looks like a greeting. Was it addressed to the victim? |
| 2:05 | 伊芙琳 | 这个应该是“小心”，以及……这个词是“被杀害”，还有“恩佐·卡”……什么？ | Here we have dangerous killed and Enzo Carr. Wait. |
| 2:13 | 根特督察 | 恩佐·卡尔博内？ | Enzo carbonbe. |
| 2:14 | 芭万女士 | 为什么我对这个名字有些熟悉？ | I think I've heard that name before. |

| 13:53 | 根特督察 | 那个时候大多数人都睡着了，劳伦斯回到房间，换上大一号的鞋子和外面捡来的脏衣服，潜入阿尔伯特的房间，然后—— | With almost everyone in the estate asleep, he could have returned to his room unnoticed. Put on his disguise, sneaked into his father's room and. |

| 14:17 | 芭万女士 | 闯入雷蒙内尔宅邸的陌生人则显然不想让任何人知道他的存在。如果是劳伦斯，他又为什么这么做？ | In contrast, it seems our intruder didn't want to be noticed at all. What would be the point of a disguise if Lawrence didn't intend to be seen? |
| 14:27 | 根特督察 | 乔装打扮也许只是劳伦斯的一个保险举措…… | As a matter of precaution, perhaps. |

| 14:43 | 根特督察 | 从用人和雷蒙内尔家的人口中都可以得知，父子之间的关系并不融洽，甚至偶尔会在经济问题上爆发严重的争执。这使得他有了作案动机。 | According to the statements, Albert romana and his son were not on the warmest of terms. They had had serious disagreements over finances in recent months. |
| 14:54 | 根特督察 | 在动机之外，劳伦斯还有完美的作案机会。想想看，做儿子的进入书房找自己的父亲，有谁会觉得这件事可疑呢？ | Lawrence also enjoyed a distinct advantage who would think twice about a son entering his own father's study? |
| 15:02 | 根特督察 | 还有最后一点——手段。阿尔伯特虽然年纪有点大了，但体格之健硕仍然不减当年，想要勒死他可不是谁都能做得到的。 | And we mustn't overlook the manner of the murder. Albert was no spring chicken, certainly, but he was as strong as an ox. Strangling him would have been no easy feat. |
| 15:13 | 根特督察 | 劳伦斯恰好有这样的能力，他曾在海外服役，体格和父亲一样强壮，而且还更年轻。为了提高成功率，他还在父亲的饮料中掺入了安眠药。 | Among all the current suspects, Lawrence alone is likely to possess the strength required. | 16:04 | 芭万女士 | 比如调查一下阿尔伯特的关系网，以及对昨夜的闯入者做一些更深入的调查。 | Check over the victim's associates and collect more information on the intruder. |

| 时间 | 说话人 | 中文（官方意译·仅供参考） | 英文（配音原声逐字听写） |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0:00 | 玛利亚 | …… | 啊？ |
| 0:01 | 玛利亚 | ……暂时还没有人注意到，不过它不能继续留在这座房子里了。 | No one has taken notice of it yet, but I cannot keep it in the manor any longer. |
| 0:07 | 玛利亚 | 哪里比较安全……？ | Is there somewhere safe? |
| 0:09 | 阿比盖尔 | 玛利亚小姐？ | Maria. |
| 0:10 | 阿比盖尔 | 她走了。 | She's left. |
| 0:12 | 玛利亚 | 你是说伊芙琳小姐？ | Miss Evelyn, do you mean? |
| 0:14 | 阿比盖尔 | 对，但是她还会回来吧？她走的时候还在说：“我会回来的。”她要怎么回来？她真的是魔法师吗？ | Yes. But she'll be returning soon, I presume. She said she would. Will she ride on a broom when she comes back? Like a real witch. |
| 0:29 | 阿比盖尔 | 还好我不喜欢我自己改过的结局，她下次来之前，我会重新画一个更好的故事。 | At least this gives me more time to refine the story. I am not at all happy with the current version. I shall be sure it's even better by the time she returns. |
| 0:40 | 阿比盖尔 | 不过我忘记问她手杖的事了，她的手杖是糖果做的吗？ | Oh. I forgot to ask her about her cane. Is it really made of sweets? |
| 0:48 | 玛利亚 | 我猜不是，不然她的手一定是黏糊糊的。或者说，你想让我也给你做一个糖果手杖，好让手心整天变得黏糊糊的？ | I think not. Otherwise her hands would be endlessly sticky. Would you like to try? We could make a cane of sweets so you can feel what it's like. |
| 1:00 | 阿比盖尔 | 啊咦——我才不要，这太脏了。 | Ew, no, how horrible. |
| 1:03 | 玛利亚 | 哈哈…… | 嘿嘿。 |
| 1:05 | 阿比盖尔 | 玛利亚小姐，今天是你休假的日子，为什么你还留在家里呢？平时在这个时候，你都会出门买东西。 | Isn't today your day off Maria? What are you doing here with me? You ought to be out shopping at this hour. |
| 1:16 | 玛利亚 | 我打算留在家陪你，明天再出门，你觉得怎么样？ | I thought I would keep you company to today, so I shall go shopping to morrow instead. How does that sound? |
| 1:23 | 阿比盖尔 | ……那倒也可以。可是不凑巧，我昨天就打算好了，今天你不在的时候我要自己读一遍《妖精和玻璃瓶》。 | 嗯。 All right. But I had a plan for today. I was going to read the spirit in a bottle. |
| 1:32 | 阿比盖尔 | 里面的词我应该都认识了。 | I believe I can read it all by myself now. |
| 1:35 | 玛利亚 | 那你又要一个人待在房间里吗？ | I see. Then shall I leave you to it. |
| 1:39 | 阿比盖尔 | 嗯。可是……晚餐的时候能见到你吗？ | HM. Yes please. Will you be joining us for dinner? |
| 1:44 | 玛利亚 | 当然，晚餐的时候我一定在家。 | Of course. I shall see you then. |
| 1:48 | 阿比盖尔 | 那我要看书了，玛利亚小姐。 | All right, see you then, Maria. |
| 1:51 | 玛利亚 | 艾比，在这之前……我还有一个问题想问你——昨天晚上你真的看见了劳伦斯吗？ | And appy. One quick question. Did you really see your brother last night? |
| 1:58 | 阿比盖尔 | 看见了呀，不是和你说过吗？ | Yes, I did. I thought I'd already told you about it. |
| 2:04 | 玛利亚 | 劳伦斯看见你了吗？ | Did he see you? |
| 2:06 | 阿比盖尔 | 应该没有，当时他正盯着自己手里的什么东西在看，没有注意到我。可他的表情很可怕……他生病了吗？ | I don't think so. He was staring at something in his hands with a rather frightening look on his face. Perhaps he wasn't feeling well. |
| 2:17 | 玛利亚 | 别担心，劳伦斯最近的工作很繁忙，应该只是有些累了。不要去打扰他，好吗？ | You needn't worry. I'm sure he was only tired from his work lately. Just leave him be, would you? |
| 2:26 | 阿比盖尔 | 嗯，反正劳伦斯也不喜欢和我说话，不过，我会听你的话的，玛利亚小姐。 | He hardly ever speaks to me anyway. But all right. I' try not to bother him since you asked me to. |
| 2:35 | 布兰奇 | ……不管怎么说，这潜艇的生意是劳伦斯的，不是我的。 | In any case, this is Lawrence's business, not mine. |

| 2:45 | 芭万女士 | 布兰奇小姐，桑德兰上尉，希望我没有打搅到你们的讨论。 你是应警方的要求过来接受调查的吗，上尉？ | Miss romanel, captain, Sunderland. I hope I haven't interrupted. Have the police asked you to cooperate with the investigation? Captain. |
| 2:54 | 桑德兰上尉 | 正是这样，女士，我刚赶过来，给警方提供了一些证词。没想到我上午离开之后没多久，阿尔伯特先生就…… | I. You have the right of it, madam. They took my statement a short while ago. To think that when I left this morning, Mr romanel was already. 啊？ |

| 3:18 | 桑德兰上尉 | 我准备把笔记送到他家里去，他们家的用人应该知道该怎么处理这些文件。 | I shall return them to his estate. His household staff are trustworthy enough. |

| 3:32 | 桑德兰上尉 | 没问题，情况特殊，任何从这个屋子里带走的东西都要小心谨慎。 | Not at all. I understand under the circumstances that it is best to exercise every caution with anything that might serve as evidence. |

| 3:55 | 布兰奇 | 劳伦斯一定告诉过你，我们可敬的父亲对他的儿女有多吝啬——一年只有不到1000英镑的生活费。 | You must have heard from Lawrence what a miser my father was. My annual allowance never exceeded a mere thousand pounds. |
| 4:03 | 布兰奇 | 最可怕的时候，我甚至要穿着同一套衣服，连续参加三场舞会——所有人都瞧不起我！ | To think that I, blanche romanel, was forced to attend three consecutive balls in the very same gown. I was the veritable laughing stock of all London. |
| 4:14 | 桑德兰上尉 | 布兰奇小姐，这……嗯，这不光是钱的问题。向深海探索最终是一门为社会谋利益的事业。 | Miss romanel. You place far too much emphasis on money. The exploration of the ocean is a public endeavor. What we are doing is for the benefit of society. |
| 4:26 | 桑德兰上尉 | 这次展览不光为我们的国家争得光荣，还能促进全人类的知识进步。在这个时代，扩展知识就是一个人最伟大的贡献。 | Our exhibition will not only bring honor to the nation. But also contribute to the intellectual advancement of the world. | 8:36 | ？？？ | ——？！ | 啊？ |

| 8:43 | 芭万女士 | 劳伦斯先生，书房现在是案发现场，禁止任何非官方人员进入。你最好对自己的行为有合理的解释。 | Mr romanel, the study has been locked down. As you well know, non staff are prohibited from entering. Care to explain yourself? |
| 8:51 | 劳伦斯 | ……我想起来一件事，警察在调查现场的时候遗漏了一些东西。但这不是他们的问题，因为这件事只有我才清楚。 | My apologies again, madame, but I believe the police have overlooked an important clue. Not that I blame them, for it is something only I am aware of. |
| 9:01 | 芭万女士 | 即便如此，你也应该先向警方提出申请。还是说，你不打算把这条被遗漏的线索交给警方？ | Even so, you should have submitted an entry request first. Are you trying to hide something from us? |
| 9:08 | 劳伦斯 | 这个，我担心…… | ✓ Well, I'm afraid... |
| 9:11 | 劳伦斯 | 都怪我太愚蠢，女士！我怕这个举动让你们觉得我是在刻意引导调查方向……我希望先确认事实，再向官方提供可靠的信息。 | Ah, I wasn't thinking clearly. I was worried the police would think I was misleading them, so I wished to confirm it before I said anything that might bring suspicion upon myself. |
| 9:22 | 芭万女士 | 在没有掌握切实证据的情况下，任何人都不会被怀疑。现在可以说说警方和我的私人侦探都遗漏了些什么吗？ | We wouldn't suspect anyone without reason, Mr Lawrence. Now. Would you care to tell me about this clue? I'm curious what both the police and my detective managed to miss. |
| 9:35 | 劳伦斯 | 是书房的保险柜。那里面本来应该有一些东西的，可今天在现场，用父亲的钥匙打开柜子之后，那些东西不见了。 | It's the safe. Something was missing from it when I opened it for the police this morning. |
| 9:40 | 劳伦斯 | 保险柜里有一个装金币和钞票的箱子，至少有三分之一的金镑消失不见了，可能还丢失了一些大面额的纸钞。 | My father always kept a box filled with gold coins and banknotes inside it. I found that roughly a third of the gold was gone, along with a few large denominations too. |
| 9:51 | 芭万女士 | 你认为是被凶手偷走的？ | And you think the murderer stole them. |
| 9:54 | 劳伦斯 | 不能排除这个可能，对吧？他为了钱财杀死了我的父亲，然后用父亲身上的钥匙打开了保险柜。 | It's a possibility, is it not? A residential robbery, the intruder would have had to kill my father because he was carrying the key. |
| 10:03 | 芭万女士 | 能确定遗失的金额有多少吗？ | Can you confirm the stolen amount? |
| 10:06 | 劳伦斯 | 我无法确定具体的数字，但那绝对不是一笔小钱，可能有上万英镑——父亲总是会把大量现金留在家里。 | Not accurately, but I'm sure it was a great deal of money, perhaps even more than 10,000 pounds. Father always kept a large sum in his safe. |
| 10:17 | 芭万女士 | 你为什么会知道保险柜里原本有什么东西？ | Why are you so familiar with its contents? |
| 10:20 | 劳伦斯 | 父亲从不避讳在我面前打开保险柜，里面的东西我大概有了解。 | Father often opened his safe in my presence. I have a rough idea of what's inside from what I've glimpsed over the years. |
| 10:28 | 劳伦斯 | 不光是财物，消失不见的还有一封信件。 | Besides money, a letter is also missing. |
| 10:32 | 劳伦斯 | 昨天下午来书房和父亲见面的时候，他正皱着眉读一封信。在我进入房间后，他就把信纸折好放在桌上。 | When I entered my father's study yesterday afternoon, I found him reading a letter. His brow was deeply furrowed, and he folded it away as soon as he noticed me. |
| 10:43 | 劳伦斯 | 我问他那封信的内容，但他没有回答，而是把它锁进了保险柜里。眼下这封信已经消失不见了。 | I asked him about its contents, but he simply put it in his safe without a word, and now it's gone. |
| 10:51 | 芭万女士 | 你有任何机会看到那封信件上的内容吗？ | Any chance you saw a word or two? |
| 10:54 | 劳伦斯 | 不，我没有看清上面写了什么，只知道应该是一封信件。 | No, all I know is that it was a letter. |
| 10:57 | 芭万女士 | 嗯……恩佐·卡尔博内，这个名字你有印象吗？ | I see. Have you ever heard of a man called Enzo carbone? |
| 11:04 | 劳伦斯 | 我听过这个名字，最近南岸发生了一场谋杀案，死者的名字就叫恩佐·卡尔博内，听说是个无亲无故的意大利人。 | Of course it was in all the newspapers at the time. Poor fellow was murdered on the south coast. They say he had no friends or family to speak of. |
| 11:14 | 劳伦斯 | 这和我们说的事有关系吗？ | Does he have some relation to this case? |
| 11:17 | 芭万女士 | 也许有，也许没有。警方的调查工作仍在进行中，请原谅我无法向你透露全部细节。 | Maybe. But since the investigation is still ongoing, I'm not allowed to divulge any details. I hope you understand. |
| 11:26 | 劳伦斯 | …… | — |
| 11:28 | 劳伦斯 | 好吧，我尊重你们的工作，可我是阿尔伯特的长子，也是现在雷蒙内尔家的主事人，我有责任和义务找到谋害父亲的凶手。 | Well, all respect to your work, but as the eldest son and new head of the romanel family. I take it as my duty. To find my father's murderer. |
| 11:39 | 劳伦斯 | 希望基金会和警方能与我有更紧密的合作。 | I hope the foundation and the police allow me to participate in the investigation. |
| 11:45 | 芭万女士 | 你打算做什么，劳伦斯先生？ | What are you planning to do Mr Roman? |
| 11:48 | 劳伦斯 | 那封信件上一定写着相当危险的内容，危险到为他惹来了杀身之祸。也许找到那封消失的信件，就能找到谋害父亲的凶手。 | If that letter was important enough to kill my father over, then I dare say it holds the truth we seek. |
| 11:56 | 劳伦斯 | 我一定要找到那封信。 | I must find it. |

## 第 6 章

| 时间 | 说话人 | 中文（官方意译·仅供参考） | 英文（配音原声逐字听写） |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0:00 | 布兰奇 | ……真是够荒唐的。 | 哼。 How ludicrous. |
| 0:03 | 玛利亚 | 我们最好在晚餐前赶回去，布兰奇。 | We'd best return before dinner time, miss Roman. |
| 0:07 | 布兰奇 | 谁这么说过？——布鲁姆？他没资格管我，也没资格把你当成他手下的一个下人支使……他什么时候能明白这点？ | According to whom? MR bloom. PH as if that dolt has the right to tell me what to do. And does he not understand that you are not under his stewardship? |
| 0:19 | 玛利亚 | 不是他，我答应过艾比，会和她一起用晚餐。 | You misunderstand, madam. I promised Abby that I would have dinner with her. |
| 0:25 | 布兰奇 | …… | 唉。 |
| 0:26 | 布兰奇 | 艾比是个讨人喜爱的孩子，虽然偶尔会有些怪脾气——呵，谁叫她姓雷蒙内尔呢。 | She's a good girl, our Abby though a tad eccentric at times. I suppose she is a romanel after all. |
| 0:35 | 布兰奇 | 陪我坐一会儿吧，玛利亚，这儿离家不远，我们随时都能赶回去。 | Sit with me for a while, Maria. We have still have time enough to return before dinner. |
| 0:41 | 布兰奇 | ……没想到一个从外面雇来的家庭教师能照顾好我的妹妹，忍受她夜游的毛病。 | If I'm perfectly honest, I hadn't expected any governess to be capable of looking after my sister properly. |
| 0:48 | 布兰奇 | 你比我懂得多，也更有耐心。我记得艾比得流感的那一次，你一直陪在她身边，差点把自己也搭进去。 | But you have proved me wrong many times. Over with your wisdom and patience. Do you recall when Abby had the flu? You hardly left her side. Putting yourself at great risk. |
| 1:03 | 玛利亚 | 最终治好疾病的是莫蒂默医生，我只是陪在她身边减少她的痛苦……还好，在老家学到的方法能起到一些作用。 | It was Dr. Mortimer who healed her. All I did was provide what I could. Luckily, I had learned how to comfort children in my hometown. |
| 1:13 | 布兰奇 | 劳伦斯一整天都在念叨着什么丢失的信件，你知道为什么吗？ | Lawrence has been looking for a missing letter today. You don't by any chance know why. |
| 1:19 | 玛利亚 | 我也不清楚他为什么会觉得那封信这么重要，为什么突然问起这个？ | I'm afraid not. Why do you ask? |
| 1:23 | 布兰奇 | 胡思乱想而已，要怪就怪劳伦斯在家里翻箱倒柜地找那张破纸。 | Just out of curiosity. My brother has rummaged his way through almost every drawer in the manor. |
| 1:31 | 玛利亚 | 所以你才想出来？ | Is that why you wished to come out for a walk? |
| 1:34 | 布兰奇 | 让人烦心的不止劳伦斯那一个。你不记得今天下午发生的事吗？ | Oh, there's more than just that to contend with. Remember what happened this afternoon. |
| 1:40 | 布兰奇 | 苏格兰场的人，医院的人，殡仪馆的人，还有律师事务所的助理，他们不知道为了什么吵个不停。 | Police, doctors, undertakers and lawyers all gathered in father's study and somehow saw it fit to begin an argument in such a place. |
| 1:50 | 布兰奇 | 他们吵架的地方就在父亲的书房前，那具尸体……那具尸体就摆在他们面前。 | To yell and yell in such a way over. Over my father's body. |
| 1:56 | 布兰奇 | 那个时候，劳伦斯在找那张*伦敦粗口*的纸！ | And all the while Laurence was faffing about looking for that god forsaken letter. |
| 2:01 | 玛利亚 | ——布兰奇，我们在教堂里。 | Miss homanel, we' are in a church. |
| 2:05 | 布兰奇 | …… | — |
| 2:09 | 布兰奇 | 对不起，玛利亚。 | I'm sorry, Maria. |
| 2:10 | 布兰奇 | 在这里瞎猜什么用也没有。也许那东西和父亲年轻时候的经历有关。 | I know it is meaningless to complain and perhaps that letter does have some connection with father's past. |
| 2:17 | 玛利亚 | 他过去……怎么了？ | His past. Why do you say that? |
| 2:22 | 布兰奇 | 一个人对自己的父亲毫无了解，是不是很奇怪？他年轻的时候去了哪里，做了什么，靠什么赚钱——我一概不知。 | Do you not think it strange that I know nothing about my father's youth. Where he had been. What he had done. How he came into his wealth. |
| 2:35 | 布兰奇 | 只记得在我十多岁的时候，他带着一大笔钱从国外回来，向全家宣布，我们要过上好日子了。 | All I know is that he returned home from abroad one day with a fortune in hand and announced that we were rich. |
| 2:44 | 布兰奇 | 母亲没有享受多久的富裕生活。差不多就在同一时间，她在生下艾比的时候难产而死……劳伦斯有好几年都在怨恨自己刚出生的妹妹。 | 嗯。 What a pity that mother didn't live long enough to enjoy it. As you well know, she died giving birth to Abby soon after. Lawrence resented her for some time because of it, though she was a mere baby and bore no blame. |
| 3:02 | 布兰奇 | 最初来照顾艾比的保姆把她当流浪猫对待。父亲总是不在家，而劳伦斯对自己小妹妹的哭闹则是有多远躲多远。 | HM. With mother gone, we had to hire nursemaids to look after Abby, but they treated her with no more care than they would a stray cat. Not that anyone one cared or noticed. Father was always away on business. And Lawrence simply ignored her cries. |
| 3:23 | 玛利亚 | 我知道之后发生了什么。你是一个好姐姐。 | I am aware of what happened later. You're a good sister. |
| 3:28 | 布兰奇 | 但我不是一个好人。 | But I am not a good person. |
| 3:31 | 玛利亚 | 什么？ | Why would you say such a thing? |
| 3:33 | 布兰奇 | 你刚来的时候我对你的态度很差，这是我的错。你替我承担照顾艾比的责任，我才有机会抽身去美国找机会。 | I was dreadfully unkind to you when you first came to us. And for that I cannot apologize enough. If you hadn't relieved me of my responsibilities. I would never have had the chance to travel to America. |
| 3:48 | 布兰奇 | 很久之前我就知道不能一辈子指望父亲过活——尤其最近几年，他变得相当吝啬。 | I learnt long ago that I could hardly rely on my father my whole life. Over the years he had become increasingly stingy with his money. |
| 3:58 | 玛利亚 | 美国是什么样的？ | 嗯。 What was America like? |
| 4:02 | 布兰奇 | 牛马成群，几百人的小镇，红褐色泥土上修到一半的铁轨，被太阳晒得滚烫的手枪。 | Herds of livestock. Small towns of a few hundred people. Half laid railways. Gunshots fired in broad daylight. |
| 4:13 | 布兰奇 | 那里刚打完仗，到处都有赚钱的机会——你想去美国吗？ | Their war ended only a few years ago. So there are opportunities everywhere. Are you interested in going yourself? |
| 4:23 | 玛利亚 | 我？不……我没有去新大陆的计划。 | To America. No, I've never considered it. |
| 4:28 | 布兰奇 | 那我希望你能留下来，劳伦斯应该不会拒绝和你签订一份新的合同。 | Then I hope you can stay. I have no doubt Lawrence would be willing to sign a new contract with you. |
| 4:36 | 玛利亚 | 我很愿意照顾艾比，直到她成年。 | I shall be more than happy to look after Abby until she comes of age. |
| 4:40 | 布兰奇 | 嗯……不管我喜不喜欢劳伦斯，这个家之后都是他的了。虽然遗产里也有我和艾比的份，但总归是少数。 | Good. Whether I like it or not, Lawrence will be the head of the family. Abby and I will get our share of the inheritance, of course, but it Shan't be much. |
| 4:52 | 布兰奇 | 父亲留下的土地、产业，还有经营特权……看他会拿这些东西怎么办吧。 | ✓ Father's land, property and business... I wonder what he'll make of them. |
| 4:56 | 布兰奇 | 人还是得靠自己，对吧，玛利亚？ | I suppose all a woman can count on is herself. Don't you agree, Maria. |
| 5:03 | 伊芙琳 | ——在哪里？！ | Where is the blasted thing? |
| 5:06 | 瓶瓶罐罐 | 呜哇—— | 哇哦。 |
| 5:08 | 伊芙琳 | 克拉伦登广场？不。边上一些的……火车站？不，不，找过了，没戏！ | Clarendon now not even close the train station? No, no, I checked that one already. |
| 5:15 | 伊芙琳 | 电缆街边上的铁路——去过！十字街的每一条小巷，还有诺克福德奶奶的帽子店……不，全都没有！ | The railway near cable street? Not there. The alleys of cross street, granny rochford's hat store. I can't find it anywhere. |
| 5:25 | 瓶瓶罐罐 | 伊芙琳，快说说你在找谁？ | Evelyn, who is it you're looking for? |
| 5:28 | 伊芙琳 | 那个大的、八条腿的，呃——怪物，线索的小偷！ | ✓ That great eight-legged beast, the thief of clues. |
| 5:33 | 瓶瓶罐罐 | 噢！那个小偷，它又把你的线索偷走了。 | Oh, stole your leads again, did he? |
| 5:37 | 瓶瓶罐罐 | 我们是你的证人，发誓绝不作伪证——那个大家伙尾随你出了门。它刚刚急匆匆地逃回家，正躲在桌子下不敢作声呢。 | As your witnesses, we swear to tell you the truth he followed you out of the door, then came hurrying back just a moment ago. ✓ He's hiding under the table as we speak. |
| 5:50 | 伊芙琳 | …… | — |
| 5:51 | ？？？ | ——喔！ | Ouch. |
| 5:54 | ？？？ | 拿根棍子猛猛敲，这才不是一个侦探的办法！ | How very rude. A True Detective who never solve puzzles through violence. |
| 6:02 | 伊芙琳 | 哈——这是对付你的办法，罪犯的帮凶——趁我不注意，不，是趁我太过注意的时候偷走我的线索。 | Ha, you deserve it, accomplice to a criminal, you'd nab my clues when I wasn't paying attention? No, when I was paying too much attention. |
| 6:11 | 谜题之王 | 我不是小偷，我是谜题之王，八条腿的谜题之王。 | I AIN't no thief, I am the great eight legged lord of puzzles. |
| 6:22 | 伊芙琳 | 我不在乎你是谁——线索，快把线索还给我。 | Ugh, do you think I care who or what you are? ⚠ Just get me my bloody clothes will you? → ✓ Do you think I care who or what you are? Just get me my bloody clues, will ya? |
| 6:29 | 谜题之王 | 一门心思想着破案，可你算得上哪门子的侦探？ | Oh you are a disgrace. Making vain attempts to solve cases. A shame to the title detective. |
| 6:40 | 谜题之王 | 今天上午那个断腿的谜题你考虑得如何——竟然是全错。可见你不是这块料呀，伊芙琳，那位可敬的同行比你聪明了至少三百倍。 | Take that leg puzzle you were given, for instance. You got it all sorts of wrong. I'd say your esteemed peer is at least 300 times as smart as you. |
| 6:55 | 伊芙琳 | 我在学习！ | Hey, that's just cause I'm still learning. |
| 6:59 | 谜题之王 | 比如说学会了看鞋印？我都不想说你在十字街的泥巴地里爬了多久才摸清里面的门道，那叫一个脏。 | At a terrible speed, might I add, you spent weeks crawling the muddy streets of cross street to master that footprint trick. Remember, you looked as ragged as a stray dog. |
| 7:15 | 谜题之王 | 再学习、再思考下去，总有一天你会彻底发疯，或者“啪”的一声，脑袋像泥浆里的死鱼嘴里吐出的最后一颗泡泡那样——破掉。 | If you go on studying and solving cases like this, you'll go balbmy one day. or worse, your head' will pop like a rotten fish baked in the sun and what a smell that would be. |
| 7:33 | 谜题之王 | 我才是更聪明、更会思考的那一个。把线索扔进我的肚子里，让它们慢慢被消化，最后变成答案！——多简单的办法。 | Course, if you don't wish for that to happen, I'd be more than obliged to take over. Simply throw the clues into my belly and after a moment's digestion I shall give you the answer. Simple and efficient, don't you think? |
| 7:50 | 伊芙琳 | 停，停一下——还提这个？不不不不不……“黄金面具案”，想得起来吗？ | Oh, you're bringing that up again, are you? No, no, no, don't you remember ✓ the golden mask case. |
| 7:59 | 伊芙琳 | 你把线索吞下去，告诉我凶手是死者的双胞胎弟弟，差点把无辜的人送上绞刑架。见鬼的方法。 | You swallowed the clues and told me that the victim was killed by his twin brother, but he was innocent. Ah, I almost led the poor bloke to the gallows. What kind of deductive method is that? |
| 8:12 | 谜题之王 | 十次里面，我只会错六次，这概率可算不上低了。 | Oh, four of my 10 guesses were correct. I'd say that's rather acceptable, wouldn't you? |
| 8:20 | 伊芙琳 | 坏心眼！你说的话我一个字都不会信。这个案子我会靠我自己来破，不会让你乱来——绝不会！ | Oh shut up, you squirmy lump of jelly. I AIN't buying a single one of your smarmy words again. No, I'm cracking the case me myself this time, so you just keep well out of it. |
| 8:35 | 伊芙琳 | 把我的——线索——还给我！ | ⚠ Now give me back my clothes. → ✓ Now, give me back my clues! |
| 8:40 | 瓶瓶罐罐 | 救命，伊芙琳！ | Help Evelyn. |
| 8:43 |  | 我才不会还你线索！伊芙琳，这都是为了你好，我的直觉告诉我，凶手就是……！ | I AIN't giving it back, Evelyn, for your own good, my intuition tells me that the murderer is. |
| 8:50 |  | 停——停停，不需要你来告诉我答案。 | Shush, all right, just shut it, I don't need your answer. |
| 8:55 |  | 距离真相只有一步之遥，何苦要为难自己？你都已经站不稳了——哦，痛！ | But you're only one step away from the truth. Why bother thinking so hard you can barely stand. Ouch. |
| 9:05 |  | 自大狂，别想搅乱——我的思路！ | Don't you interrupt me when I'm fainting. |
| 9:08 |  | 别跑，小偷，把东西交出来！ | ⚠ Hold it, you rogue, get me back, me clues. → ✓ Hold it, you rogue! Get me back me clues! |
| 9:12 |  | 你这家伙打起架来和你的脑子一样疯癫，你都不知道疼的吗？……别打我的头！ | Hitting me is only hitting yourself, you lunatic. Don't it hurt me. |
| 9:20 |  | 你的——头？哈，我的头！别自以为是地评价我的头！ | Your head, that's my head, you rusty old bucket, and I can do whatever I want with it. |
| 9:27 | 谜题之王 | 噢——噢！这一招太疼了。 | Ouch, that hurts. |
| 9:31 | 伊芙琳 | 我会把你拆碎的，快把你的秘密交出来。 | Give me my clues or I'll tear you limb from limb. |
| 9:35 | 谜题之王 | 不——！ | No. |
| 9:38 | 谜题之王 | 疼呀！ | 吼吼吼吼。 |
| 9:41 | 伊芙琳 | 你这铁疙瘩根本不——会——疼！ | Quit pretending, will yer. I know you don't feel a thing, you bucket of bolts. |
| 9:47 | 谜题之王 | 我亮晶晶的秘密—— | My clues, my sparkling clues. |
| 9:52 | 伊芙琳 | 走开。 | Get lost. |
| 9:54 | 谜题之王 | 唔…… | 哦。 |
| 9:57 | 伊芙琳 | ……！就是这个，被偷走的线索。 | That's it, the clues are in there. |
| 10:01 | 伊芙琳 | …… | 啊？ |
| 10:04 | 伊芙琳 | ………… | 唉。 啊？ |
| 10:08 | 伊芙琳 | 这是——学习室里的书柜？玛利亚开门的时候，我在门口张望的那一个瞬间看到了它……线索是那时候被偷走的？ | 哎。 Is this the bookcase in Abigail's schoolroom? I caught a glimpse of it when Maria opened the door. Is that when the clues were stolen then? |
| 10:23 | 伊芙琳 | 不管了，快想想…… | Never mind it, just focus. |
| 10:26 | 伊芙琳 | 最顶上的三层书籍都是一些落灰的大部头，把书柜塞得满满当当的，只有倒数第二高的那一层比较稀疏。 | The top three shelves of the bookcase were jammed with books. Only the second lower shelf had some space. |
| 10:32 | 伊芙琳 | 如果书太少，塞不满书柜，一般也不至于留下这一层而把上下两层都装满。 | If there weren't enough books to fill the whole of it, you'd either fill it from top to bottom or fill it from bottom to top, not leave a great chunk in the middle. |
| 10:41 | 伊芙琳 | 倒像是有人从这一层拿走了什么东西，为了填补空缺而重新排列了书籍——还有不少灰尘被手指擦掉的印记，只能是最近的事。 | Ah, it looked as if something had been taken from the shelf here and the books had been rearranged to cover it up. The areas where the dust had been wiped away were still clearly visible, so it must have happened recently. |
| 10:56 | 伊芙琳 | 只有经常使用这个房间的人，才会在书柜上存放物品。阿比盖尔？她的身高够不到，那更可能是—— | Only two people are likely to have used this room in the bookcase inside, Abigail's too short to reach it, which leaves us with. |
| 11:05 | 伊芙琳 | 玛利亚。 | Maria erb. |

| 时间 | 说话人 | 中文（官方意译·仅供参考） | 英文（配音原声逐字听写） |

| 0:12 | 玛利亚 | 他知道，我要到运河对面去，之前从那儿的书商手里订了几本儿童百科全书。这里发生什么了？ | He is. I am to cross the canal to fetch the encyclopaedias I ordered for miss romanel. What happened here? |
| 0:22 | 警员 | 那你得绕路了，难道你没有听说过“水巷谋杀案”吗？ | You'll have to take a detour, I'm afraid. Haven't you heard about the water lane murder? |

| 0:31 | 警员 | 那你一定读得不够仔细，不然就不会不知道，你现在面朝的巷子正是案发现场。 | Then you must have passed over the details, otherwise you would have known that this alley is the scene of the crime. |
| 0:38 | 玛利亚 | 我只记得是靠近运河的某条巷子，真没想到就是我平时会路过的地方。 | I had noted that it was some alley or other near the canal. Though I suppose I ought to have considered that it was my usual crossing. |
| 0:49 | 玛利亚 | 可现在距离案发时间应该已经过去了一周多，现场应该也已经清理过了，为什么现在又被封锁了？ | But it's been over a week since then. Surely the police ought to have cleaned up the scene by now. Where' you let off again? |
| 0:58 | 警员 | 我们有了一些新的发现，根特督察要求我们重新调查一下现场，看有没有什么遗漏的东西。 | A new lead opened up. Miss inspector gent ordered us to search the crime scene again in case we missed something. |
| 1:04 | 玛利亚 | 根特督察也在调查这起案子？ | Then I suppose he's also in charge of this case. |

| 1:15 | 警员 | 小姐？你在听我说话吗？ | Miss, did you hear me? |

| 1:26 | 警员 | 对呀，这不是你该来的地方，快走吧。 | That it is, miss, no place for a young lady such as yourself. Off you go then. |

| 1:34 | 玛利亚 | ………… | — |
| 1:43 | 落魄的工人 | 嘿，嘿，走开！ | Hey, hey, shove off. |
| 1:46 | 玛利亚 | ……嗯？ | 嗯。 |

| 2:02 | 玛利亚 | ……唉。 | 哎。 |
| 2:04 | 玛利亚 | ——先生，请不要慌张，我来帮你赶走它们。 | Come down Siri, help you get rid of them. |
| 2:07 |  | 哦……？！小姐，请小心——！嘿，别翻我的口袋！ | ✓ Hey! Don't go through my pockets!. |

| 2:26 |  | 没什么，这些小家伙平常不会这样，你有没有受伤？ | I am perfectly fine, sir. Though it seems the carbuncles are especially naughty to today. Are you hurt? |

| 2:36 | 玛利亚 | 它们把你的什么东西给抢走了？ | What have they taken from you? |
| 2:39 | 落魄的工人 | 我的午饭。现在我该吃什么呀！ | Oh MY dinner. What am I supposed to eat now, eh? |
| 2:43 | 玛利亚 | 我身上也没有能吃的东西，给，请收下这些。 | I'm afraid I don't have any food on my person, but here, please take this. |
| 2:49 | 落魄的工人 | 善良的小姐，这够我买三天的面包了。可您千万别把我当成乞丐了，我是靠自己的双手和头脑生活的。 | Oh MY, AIN't you a real brick? This'll buy me three days worth of grub, let alone a meal, I AIN't no beggar, though. And my pride won't allow me to simply take this from you for nothing. |
| 3:01 | 落魄的工人 | 我之后赚到了钱，一定把它们还给您。 | So I'll be sure to pay you back as soon as I get me wages. Promise. |
| 3:06 | 玛利亚 | 我相信你会这么做的，先生，你是刚来这个码头的吗？ | I believe you will. You're new to the stock, I take it. I don't believe I have seen you before. |
| 3:13 | 落魄的工人 | 对，两天前才来伦敦的，投奔我堂哥。但是我还没联系上他，就先来运河边找点活干。 | That's right. Came up to London two days ago. My cousin lives here but I AIN't been able to find him yet. So I reckon I'd best find employment first. |
| 3:23 | 落魄的工人 | 我之前就在伯明翰的运河上工作，心想熟门熟路，没想到这里有这么多强盗魔精！ | I used to work on the Birmingham canal, so I figured it'd be much the same working here. Didn't see them critters coming though. Bunch of bloody robbers they are. |

| 3:39 | 落魄的工人 | 运河……什么互助会？没听过，在伯明翰的时候也没有这种东西。 | The canal? What? Never heard of such a thing. Nothing of that sort in Birmingham neither. |
| 3:45 | 玛利亚 | 这是一个运河工人之间互相帮助的组织，不过目前的范围还只能覆盖摄政运河上游的一小段。 | It is an organ organization which provides support for canal workers. Though its influence is currently limited to a small section of the upper reaches of the regent's canal. |
| 3:58 | 玛利亚 | 生病和受伤的工人可以在互助会内得到救治和休养，我们也会为难以为继的人提供食物和日用品，对了，那儿偶尔还会开设识字课。 | We provide treatment and respite for injured and sick workers, as well as food and daily necessities for those in need. Sometimes there are literacy classes too. |
| 4:10 | 落魄的工人 | 这听起来真是太棒了。我能在那儿找到您，对吗？那我就不怕没机会还钱了。 | Sounds terrific. I can find you there, can't I? Then I can settle up with you. |
| 4:16 | 玛利亚 | 没错，可惜今天不是聚会的日子，但我可以带你去互助会成员经常碰面的地方看看。需要为你介绍一些工作吗？ | That's right. Most members are out today. But I can show you around our offices. I can also help you secure employment if you like. |
| 4:27 | 落魄的工人 | 好心的小姐，今天我无法领受您更多的恩惠了。 | Thank you kindly, mum, but you've given me much more than enough already. |

| 4:56 | 阿尔伯特 | 我该从哪里说起？嗯……你应该想知道运河工人互助会的事，我可以从这里开始。 | Where should I begin? Well, I suppose you'd like to know about the regent's canal friendly society first. I shall start from there. |
| 5:12 | 阿尔伯特 | 每个月，我会从银行中取出500英镑，把它们兑换成硬币，让我信得过的人送去给我在摄政运河的接头人。 | Every month I withdraw 500 pounds from my accounts and have at hand delivered to my contacts at the regent's canal. |
| 5:22 | 阿尔伯特 | 他会把这些钱换成食物、服装和药品，分发给最穷苦的码头工人。 | The funds to buy food, clothing and medicine to be distributed among the laborers in need. |
| 5:30 | 阿尔伯特 | 这些支出几乎不会伤及我的财富分毫。即使有人每个月从我这里偷走500英镑，我估计也察觉不了。但是它们能帮助成百上千名工人活下去。 | It is an insignificant sum for someone of my position. I should hardly notice for someone to steal the amount from me every month, but it saved hundreds of lives nonetheless. |
| 5:43 | 阿尔伯特 | 可我不能亲自出面做这些事，也不能盲目地扩大施舍的规格。一个对穷人友好的富商会遭受同阶层的人的非议。 | The greatest limitation was that I could not step forward to handle matters personally, nor could I easily expand its scale. Charity is one thing, being seen to be mixing with the poor is another. |
| 5:59 | 阿尔伯特 | 即使他们明面上会赞扬你的慷慨，但私下里还是会用另一套说辞讨论你，而且会想尽办法拆散任何成规模的工人团体。 | They may publicly speak your praises, but behind closed doors they would whisper that you were promoting pauperism, a convenient pretext, I'd say, for their fear of the labouring classes combining. |
| 6:14 | 阿尔伯特 | …… | 啊？ |
| 6:17 | 阿尔伯特 | 1868年9月的一天，我乘坐汽船，经由运河前往一家工厂中视察。我看见一只从河岸上抛出的包裹划过半空，眼看就要落在我们船上。 | In September 1868 while travelling by steamboat to inspect a factory, a package was thrown upon the deck from the canal bank. |
| 6:28 | 阿尔伯特 | 当时的我过久了太平的日子，竟然没在第一时间意识到发生了什么。很快，我眼前发生了一场巨大的爆炸。 | Peace had reigned in this city for so long that I took little notice of it, but in a moment the package exploded. |
| 6:40 | 阿尔伯特 | 警察没有找到扔炸药的人，但我一直都怀疑是杰克·伯努森。我为了低价收购他的制药公司，想办法搞垮了他……但这并不重要。 | The police never identified the attacker, but I have always suspected it was Jacob nussen. I had engineered the downfall of his chemist's business so that I might purchase it for a fraction of its worth. |
| 6:54 | 阿尔伯特 | 我下一次睁眼的时候，已经躺在了一家干净的诊所里，身边正在发生一场争执。一群衣衫褴褛、浑身浸湿的人正在和医院雇员推搡。 | By the time I had opened my eyes again, I found myself lying in a clinic with a group of scruffy, water soaked young men arguing with the staff by my bed. |
| 7:08 | 阿尔伯特 | 我从争吵声中听出来，是那些人在爆炸发生后把我从脏污的河水中捞出来，此刻他们只想从我手上讨要几便士，好对得起他们的付出。 | I learned from their argument that they were all canal laborers. They had saved my life and wanted money in return. |
| 7:18 | 阿尔伯特 | 在那些码头工人中，一双直勾勾盯着我看的眼睛让我无法忽视——那双眼睛属于一个几乎还是孩子的人，其中没有任何的天真与善良…… | One among them was little more than a boy. He stared me in the eyes so unwaveringly that it stunned me. I did not see the innocence or kindness of a child. |
| 7:37 | 阿尔伯特 | 只有一种干巴巴的仇恨。而这份仇恨令我恐惧万分，因为那和年轻时的我一模一样。在那一瞬间，我完全明白他心中所想的事。 | I saw only hatred, the same kind I held as a youth. And that is what truly frightened me, for I knew precisely what filled his thoughts in that moment. |
| 7:55 | 阿尔伯特 | 于是我叫停了争执，翻出身上还没有被水冲走的钱币，全部交给了救下我的人——这就是运河工人互助会的起源。 | So I halted the argument and gave every Penny I had in my pockets to those young men, and later I founded the regent's canal friendly society. |
| 8:09 | 阿尔伯特 | 这不是一个发现了善良与学会感恩的故事，是吧？因为我的本性也不是一个这样的人。 | I know what you are thinking. This is not a story which reflects the goodness of man. I was neither kind hearted nor grateful by nature. |
| 8:22 | 阿尔伯特 | 我是个可耻的人。如果我必须去做有损我利益的事，那通常是受到恐惧的驱使。 | Quite the contrary, I was a shameful egotist who would never do a good turn unless my life were threatened. |
| 8:31 | 阿尔伯特 | ——如同此时此刻。 | The same is true even now. |

## 第 9 章

| 时间 | 说话人 | 中文（官方意译·仅供参考） | 英文（配音原声逐字听写） |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0:00 | 芭万女士 | 灰修士船坞？ | Dust fryers dock. |
| 0:02 | 伊芙琳 | 没错。 | That's right. |
| 0:03 | 伊芙琳 | 我要承认，这确实是一个——哦，温度高了点——确实是一个很可能被选择的躲藏地点。 | I've got to say, that place'd make for a splendid hide out for our murderer, I' blast it, temperature's a bit too high. |
| 0:12 | 伊芙琳 | 灰修士船坞不光离雷蒙内尔宅邸很近，还一直是运河上知名的藏污纳垢之地。嗯……？黏合剂太少了？ | Not only is it close to the romanel's estate, but it's known to be a haven for all kinds of scoundrels. 嗯。 Ugh, it still AIN't sticky enough. |
| 0:26 | 伊芙琳 | 四年前有一场“古籍伪造案”，主谋就躲在这个船坞里。这个案子从一开始就破了，可大部分时间都花在从船坞里找人了。 | Four years ago there was a case they called the ancient text forgery they figured the culprit out in a matter of hours, but it took the coppers weeks to track the ringleader cause he was hidin out at those docks. |
| 0:42 | 伊芙琳 | 当时根特还没当上督察，他的腿结结实实吃了一颗枪子。 | Mr gents wasn't yet an inspector then took a shot to the leg in the operation. |
| 0:47 | 芭万女士 | 你说你亲眼见到玛利亚和一个自称是运河工人互助会的组织有联系？ | Getting down to business. Is it true that miss erb is involved with an organization by the name of the regent's canal friendly society? |
| 0:57 | 伊芙琳 | 没错，要是我的耳朵没有骗我——那些工人说互助会的真正赞助人正是阿尔伯特。 | ⚠ Certainly is, so I hearderd it me myself. If I AIN't mistaken, that organization was sponsored by Mr romanel. → ✓ Certainly is. So unheard of myself. If I ain't mistaken, that organisation was sponsored by Mr Romenel. |
| 1:05 | 芭万女士 | 阿尔伯特，我们的被害人？我不知道雷蒙内尔家的掌权人有这样一副好心肠。 | Albert romanel, the victim. I didn't realize he was so kind hearted. |
| 1:11 | 伊芙琳 | 好心肠？——看起来不像，但我向来接受一切可能性。 | Kind hearted? I wouldn't say so with what I've seen, but I've been proved wrong before. |
| 1:19 | 芭万女士 | 咳咳……我差点哭出来，伊芙琳。这次又是什么？ | Oh. that's quite the smell you've concocted, Evelyn. What is it? |
| 1:25 | 伊芙琳 | 浓缩了六颗马拉盖塔辣椒、两颗鸟眼椒和一整棵辣根，然后用鱼鳔胶做黏合剂的追逐用特制糖果。 | ⚠ A special sweet made with six smalllllogueta peppers, two bird's eye chilliess and an entire horseradish all glued together with ground fish. Morew. → ✓ Made with six molligetta peppers, two bird's-eye chilies, and an entire horseradish, all glued together with ground fishmaw. |
| 1:41 | 伊芙琳 | 对了，还有一大勺咖啡粉末。今天晚上可得打起精神。 | Oh and see, as we might be in for a tastest to tonight, I added a spoonful of ground coffee too. |
| 1:48 | 芭万女士 | 今天晚上？ | A chase. |
| 1:49 | 伊芙琳 | 当然是去抓捕嫌犯，你认真听了吗？他今天晚上就要乘船逃走。 | Don't you pay attention? We're going after the suspect. He's leaving on a steamboat tonight. |
| 1:56 | 伊芙琳 | 我猜他要搭乘汽船前往伦敦港，然后再换乘其他大船离开英国。中途会在莱姆豪斯换乘吗？……不，不重要，我们要在那之前抓住他。 | He's headed for the port Of London where he'll catch a ship out of England. HMM, might change boats at the limehouse actually. Never mind, we'll have him now before then. |
| 2:06 | 芭万女士 | 给根特督察发电报，让他早些组织警力拦截嫌犯的船只？毕竟我们知道他会搭乘“泥鳅号”逃亡。 | Shall we send a telegram to have the inspector intercept the steamboat? Since we already know he's taken the mudfish. |
| 2:13 | 伊芙琳 | 电报已经发出去了，但要按根特想的那么去做，一定只会扑一个空。 | HMM, ✓ already sent, ma'am, but he's planning to send a load of his men rushing in on the place. It's bound to fail. |
| 2:21 | 伊芙琳 | 任何搜查人员靠近船坞，都会引发船坞居民的警惕。嫌犯听见风声，一定会改变计划。在河道上埋伏警方船只，结果也是一样。 | As soon as the coppers approached the dock, our suspect will be alerted and change his plan them set up. An ambush won't change nothin neither. |
| 2:30 | 芭万女士 | 你有其他想法？ | I suppose you have a better idea then. |
| 2:32 | 伊芙琳 | 猜对了，何不拥抱现成的新技术！雷蒙内尔家的“阿尔戈号”不就好端端地停在他们的私人码头里吗？ | MY, you are clever, course I do, we'll use the power of science the romanel submarine, the Argo is resting at their private dock. AIN't that right? |
| 2:44 | 伊芙琳 | 它潜在水里，只露出潜望镜，绝对没有人能发现。等到嫌犯搭乘的船驶出码头，我们就浮出水面，让“阿尔戈”一口吞了小“泥鳅”。 | I say we board it, put ourselves below the water and use its periscope to observe the dock. The suspect won't have a clue what's in him. |
| 2:53 | 伊芙琳 | 根特可以向雷蒙内尔家借用潜艇。于情于理，他们都没有理由拒绝——我们可是要去抓捕杀害雷蒙内尔家主的嫌犯呀。 | The romanelves oughtn't to turn down the inspector, as long as he asks politely, we are going after their father's murderer, after all. |
| 3:02 | 伊芙琳 | 好了，计划就是这样。你要和我一起去，还是在基金会等我们的消息？ | So that's the full of it. You coming with me or returning to your office? |
| 3:06 | 芭万女士 | 我看你已经忘记要我去基金会翻一翻旧档案的事了，而且我向来对那些大铁皮盒子不感冒——祝你们好运吧。 | I suppose you've already forgotten that you asked me to check the victim's connections and enemies. Besides, I've never been a fan of those metal death traps. Best of luck. |
| 3:17 | 根特督察 | 搞定了，真是费了我好一番口舌。 | All sorted, though it took an awful lot of convincing. |
| 3:21 | 伊芙琳 | 我就知道你能做到。 | Ha. ha, I knew you could do it, me old chum. |
| 3:25 | 伊芙琳 | 可敬的船长用什么理由搪塞你呢？ | I'm curious though, what was captain Sunderland's reasoning for turning you down at first? |
| 3:32 | 根特督察 | 桑德兰上尉说“阿尔戈号”刚完成深潜任务，需要保养和维护，现在并不在最好的状态。 | He said the submarine was not in its best condition, still under maintenance after its last dive. |
| 3:38 | 伊芙琳 | 潜艇回港至少有一个月了，我不知道它的保养会这么困难。 | It's been over a month since then AIN't it? How long could a bit of maintenance possibly take? |
| 3:44 | 伊芙琳 | ……另一件事如何了？玛利亚有没有给警方传递任何有关嫌犯的信息？或者是否有任何寄到苏格兰场的匿名信？ | HMM. Anyway, what about miss erb? Did she tell the police what she heard about the suspect? Or have any anonymous letters reached the yard? |
| 3:53 | 根特督察 | 没有。玛利亚没有把这个消息分享给警方……如果她不是凶手，为什么不这么做呢？ | No. She's kept it to herself for now. Though I failed to understand what reason she might have for doing so. For she herself being the killer. |
| 4:03 | 伊芙琳 | 先忘了玛利亚吧，看看它——多么壮观。 | Oh, we'll have to talk on that later, oh my eyes, what a beauty, how splendid this can be. |
| 4:13 | 桑德兰上尉 | 根特督察，伊芙琳小姐，请登船吧，小心不要从甲板上滑下去了。 | Inspector gents, miss Evelyn, please come aboard. Take care when you step onto the deck. |
| 4:19 | 伊芙琳 | 来吧，这样的机会可不多呀，根特。 | Come on inspector, AIN't every day you get the chance to board a submarine. |
| 4:25 | 伊芙琳 | 里面还真不小。 | ✓ Blimey, there's plenty of room in here. |
| 4:30 | 桑德兰上尉 | 小心些，不要胡乱敲。你会碰到不该碰的东西的。 | Please, you mustn't go touching things willy nilly, otherwise you might get us all in a great deal of trouble. |
| 4:37 | 伊芙琳 | 难道你们平时从来没有在这个地方磕到过脑袋吗？ | What are you telling me? You AIN't never bumped your head in here, not even once. |
| 4:43 | 桑德兰上尉 | 我们知道哪里该碰，哪里不该碰。“阿尔戈号”的设计十分紧凑，有些开关和按钮就藏在不显眼的地方。 | We are always careful with our movements, for the Argo has a variety of switches and buttons that are tucked away in inconspicuous places. |
| 4:52 | 根特督察 | 你们在说什么呢？ | What are you two talking about? |
| 4:55 | 伊芙琳 | 一些技术性的问题，根特。 | HMM, technology inspector. |
| 4:58 | 根特督察 | 现在不是聊技术的时候，我们也不需要理解这艘船是怎么动起来的。还是快出发吧，桑德兰上尉，去预定的拦截地点。 | There's no time for lessons now. Best we leave this to the professionals captain to the interception site if you will. |
| 5:06 | 桑德兰上尉 | 詹姆，下潜！ | James, take us down. |
| 5:09 | 伊芙琳 | 喔——！你的水手没有认真清扫船舱啊，桑德兰上尉，地上这些滑溜溜的东西是什么？漏油了？ | Oh, captain, it seems your sailors have been shirking their duties. ✓ This slime is slipperier than a pickpocket. |
| 5:19 | 桑德兰上尉 | 哦，那只是一些魔精表皮的分泌物。 | ✓ Oh, that's just some secretion from a critter's skin. |
| 5:21 | 伊芙琳 | 魔精？在潜艇上？ | What? You're keeping critters in here. |
| 5:25 | 桑德兰上尉 | 是一条从深海中捕捞上来的软体魔精，是过去从未发现过的新品种，它本来也要作为“亚特兰蒂斯展览”的展品出展。 | Not typically, but this was a special case. We' caught a specimen of what appeared to be an undiscovered species of mollusc on a previous deep sea dive. We were hoping to showcase it at the exhibition. |
| 5:37 | 桑德兰上尉 | 可惜它在我们上岸的时候就死了。也许是它不能适应陆地的气压，罗西旺爵士对它做过研究。 | But it perished when we went to shore sir roseworn suspected the change in atmospheric pressure was to blame. |
| 5:44 | 伊芙琳 | 是昨天落在雷蒙内尔家的研究笔记上的图画？ | HMM, was that mentioned in the notes he left at The Roman house? |
| 5:48 | 桑德兰上尉 | 你怎么知道这本笔记？不过没错，就是你说的这样。 | You know about that, do you? But I it was. |
| 5:53 | 桑德兰上尉 | 罗西旺爵士对这只魔精的死亡感到十分遗憾，但这也激发了他探险的热情，不然他也不会一大早就乘船离开英国了。 | Sir rosewarne was saddened by the loss, but more than anything he was inspired. Otherwise he would not have set off on the new adventure first thing in the morning. |
| 6:03 | 桑德兰上尉 | ……不说这些了，我们很快就会抵达目标地点。 | That will do for idle chatter. We're approaching the interception site. |
| 6:07 | 桑德兰上尉 | 船舱内的氧气不多，如果嫌犯不按时出现，那我们就不得不上浮补充氧气了。根特督察，你知道怎么使用潜望镜吗？ | And please note that oxygen is limited in the cabin. If we wait too long, we shall have to ascend for replenishment. Inspector are you at all familiar with periscopes? |
| 6:18 | 根特督察 | 潜望镜？你说的是我身边的这根捅到天花板上的管子？ | Periscopes? I suppose you mean that tube suspended from the ceiling. |
| 6:23 | 根特督察 | …… | 哼。 |
| 6:25 | 根特督察 | 是——“泥鳅号”！我看见它了！ | That's the mudfish. I see it. |
| 6:29 | 伊芙琳 | 终于来了！ | Finally. |
| 6:31 | 根特督察 | 越来越近了，“泥鳅号”很快就会从我们头顶飞过去。 | It's getting closer. ✓ It'll pass right over us any minute. |
| 6:35 | 伊芙琳 | 那是游过去——不能让它跑了，快上浮拦住它。 | Boats don't fly, they sail, wait, we've got to do something, get up there and stop it. |
| 6:44 | 桑德兰上尉 | 这里的河道太窄了，这个时候上浮我们很可能会撞在一起。 | There isn't enough space for us to ascend. The chance of collision is too great. |
| 6:50 | 伊芙琳 | 我相信“阿尔戈号”更耐撞。 | Oh, I'm sure the Argo can handle a little bump. |
| 6:53 | 桑德兰上尉 | 不，我们可以掉头去追。 | Certainly not. We shall wait until they pass, then turn around and give chase. |
| 6:59 | 根特督察 | 不可能，“泥鳅号”的速度太快了，比我们警队的汽船还要快不少，我们掉过头以后它绝对已经消失不见了。 | That's impossible, the mudfish is even faster than the police's steamboat, by the time we turn around, they shall be long gone. |
| 7:08 | 根特督察 | 伊芙琳说得没错，上浮，横在河道里，我们有机会拦住它。 | Evelyn's right. This may be our only chance to stop them. We have to surface right this instant. |
| 7:15 | 桑德兰上尉 | 我拒绝这么做，这是—— | ✓ I refuse. This submarine— |
| 7:18 | 根特督察 | 是，这艘潜艇是雷蒙内尔家的资产，现在正需要它发挥价值，找到谋害阿尔伯特的凶手——而阿尔伯特刚好是你可敬的赞助人！ | Yes, yes, we all know it's The Roman all's property, but is it not also fitting that it serve as a tool to find the murderer of its honourable sponsor? |
| 7:28 | 根特督察 | 上浮！否则我会让你去苏格兰场好好解释这次妨碍公务的行为。 | Ascend now or ✓ I'll have you explain this at Scotland Yard our operation. |
| 7:33 | 桑德兰上尉 | ……哼！ | 哼。 |
| 7:35 | 桑德兰上尉 | 那就一起下地狱好了。 | Then let us descend unto hell together. |
| 7:38 | 伊芙琳 | ——！ | 啊？ |
| 7:39 | 桑德兰上尉 | 该死，我们撞上去了！ | For heaven's sake, did I not say we would crash? You fools. |
| 7:44 | 根特督察 | 这是——漏水了？ | Is that a leak? |
| 7:46 | 伊芙琳 | 快让我出去！ | ✓ Open the hatch, quick. |
| 7:49 |  | 你、你是怎么从河水里出来的？！别挡我的路！滚开！ | ✓ How did you come out of the river water?, just get out of my way. |
| 7:54 |  | 要么你换一个看法——我不是来抓你的，而是来给你一个机会自证清白的，这样会好受点吗？ | Think of it this way, sir. This AIN't an arrest, it's an opportunity to prove your innocence. Bet you feel better about it now, eh? |
| 8:04 |  | 疯女人，想骗我，门儿都没有——我什么都没做！ | I'm not falling for your tricks, I didn't do anything. |
| 8:09 |  | 至少现在不是“什么都没做”——嘿，逃跑究竟对谁有好处？配合些吧先生，别给大家添麻烦呀！ | Well, you're intentionally obstructing the line of the law and running away. That certainly won't do you any good. Just save us the trouble and turn yourself in, will you? |
| 8:20 |  | 走开！ | Leave me alone. |
| 8:22 |  | 花招真多，好吧先生，过几条街再见！ | You've sure got a fair few tricks up your sleeve, Mister, all right, then I'll be seeing you in a few streets. |
| 8:28 | 伊芙琳 | 还想往哪里跑，先生？ | You AIN't getting away from me, Mister. |
| 8:32 | 嫌犯 | 滚开！ | Go to hell. |

| 10:42 | 根特督察 | 对了，布兰奇还在睡前向用人要了几颗安眠药，而我们在阿尔伯特的杯子里也发现了安眠药的残留…… | Don't forget that miss romanel had asked for two sleeping pills before she retired. The very same kind of pill was found in the victim's drink. |
| 10:53 | 伊芙琳 | 用安眠药麻醉阿尔伯特后行凶？但布兰奇要安眠药的时间在见到罗伯特之前，如果是临时合谋，时间对不上。 | You mean she slipped the drug into Mr romanell's drink to knock him out? But she asked for the pills before Robert showed himself. Surely she couldn't have predicted his intrusion. |
| 11:04 | 伊芙琳 | 要是他们早有预谋，布兰奇也大可不必把同伙请到家里来作案。冒这个风险毫无必要。 | And it's nigh on impossible that they had plotted this in advance, otherwise they would likely have committed the murder somewhere away from her own home. |
| 11:13 | 伊芙琳 | 还有保险柜里被偷走的文件…… | Besides, we still haven't taken the stolen letter into consideration. |
| 11:18 | 根特督察 | 也许他们只是想从文件里找出阿尔伯特的遗嘱，确认自己能获得的遗产？ | Perhaps they ransacked the safe in hopes of finding the victim's will. |
| 11:23 | 伊芙琳 | 在杀人之后才想起来确认遗嘱？那他们真得祈祷幸运女神亲临英格兰了。 | After the victim was dead and couldn't possibly have changed a thing. ✓ Then they'd best pray for luck to smile on England. |
| 11:32 | 伊芙琳 | 别灰心，根特，布兰奇确实有强烈的杀人动机，只是我们缺少关键的证据。 | Oh, don't look so glum, I was only having you on miss romanell' is a perfectly good motive for the murder. We just need harder evidence. |
| 11:42 | 根特督察 | 也许这些证据对你来说还不够，可对陪审团来说，这位美国人的证词已经足够有力。 | I understand that The American statement proves little to you, but it would be quite enough to convince a jury. |
| 11:49 | 根特督察 | 至于遗产……我们可以等律师宣布阿尔伯特的遗嘱之后，跟踪布兰奇的活动，寻找更明确的证据。 | In regard to the will and inheritance, it may serve us well to monitor miss romanma's financial movements after the reading of the will. |
| 11:58 | 伊芙琳 | 好主意，我向来尊重你的判断——尤其是当你和我想到一块儿去的时候。 | On that, we're agreed. You know, I always have thought highly of your judgment, provided it's the same as mine. |
| 12:05 | 根特督察 | 我们联系了阿尔伯特的律师，特里威廉先生，他说明天就会宣布遗嘱。 | Uh. We've already inquired about the status of the will with the victim's attorney, Mr trevelyan. He said it will be read tomorrow. |
| 12:14 | 伊芙琳 | 好吧，好吧！说不定明天之后就会真相大白呢。 | Right, right. Perhaps it will shed some light. |
| 12:17 | 伊芙琳 | 至于我呢——就算死者在地下变成白骨，犯人被吊上绞刑架，在找到真相之前，推理都不能结束。 | As for me, even if the dead's long buried and the supposed culprit's been sent to the gallows, I'll keep on reasoning until I find the truth. The whole truth. |
| 12:27 | 伊芙琳 | 哦，糟了！ | Oh blimey. |
| 12:30 | 伊芙琳 | ——玛利亚小姐是不是还被关在这里？ | We forgot about miss herb. She's still in the cell, AIN't she. |

| 0:00 | 阿尔伯特 | ……嗯，你不明白的是，为什么我会恐惧那个从爆炸中救下我、眼中充满仇恨的男孩。 | WHY, you ask, would I fear a lowly boy who helped save my life? |
| 0:08 | 阿尔伯特 | 光看一眼我就知道，他孩子的外表下有成为罪犯的潜质。但我在乎的不是他。呵，我从不在乎任何人。 | Because I saw the purity of the hatred in his eyes, the unmistakable mark of an exceptionally ruthless criminal. But he was not someone I truly cared about, buth I haven' ever cared about anyone. |
| 0:26 | 阿尔伯特 | ……除了阿比盖尔，我的女儿。 | No one excepts Abigail, my dearest daughter. |
| 0:35 | 阿尔伯特 | 我从诊所回家后，在院子里看见了阿比盖尔。她不是来迎接我的，因为她还不知道我遇袭的事。 | When I returned home from the clinic, I saw Abby in the front garden. She knew nothing of my sort so, and naturally she did not come to the door to welcome me. |
| 0:49 | 阿尔伯特 | 她在一旁的草坪里摆弄着什么，我走过去，让她告诉我她在做什么。 | She was sitting on the grass playing intently with something or other, so I walked over and asked what she was doing. |
| 0:59 | 阿尔伯特 | 一只麻雀死在她的手中。 | That is when I saw the ragged corpse of a sparrow in a ball. |
| 1:05 | 阿尔伯特 | 她面无表情地掂着温度尚存的动物尸体，好像那只是一个被损坏的玩具，而她对它为什么会“坏掉”感到大惑不解。 | She was tossing it as if it were nothing more than a toy that she had unknowingly broken. |
| 1:15 | 阿尔伯特 | 阿比盖尔说，她只是想知道在拔掉多少根羽毛之后，这只麻雀才飞不起来。 | She told me she had been trying to determine how many feathers she'd have to pluck before the sparrow could no longer fly. |
| 1:28 | 阿尔伯特 | 死掉的麻雀强迫我去面对一个我一直不愿意承认的事实——阿比盖尔和我、和她的兄长与姐姐一样，也是一个雷蒙内尔。 | That scene forced me to face a reality I had refused to see. Abby is a Romano to the core. Just like her siblings. Just like myself. |
| 1:45 | 阿尔伯特 | 我的父亲就死在绞刑架上，而我的爷爷是一名海盗。 | My father was taken to the gallows for his many crimes, my grandfather was a notorious pirate. The list goes on. |
| 1:56 | 阿尔伯特 | 他们把诅咒留给了后代。雷蒙内尔永远心无所依、残酷无情，只有在与法律和道德对抗的时候才能品尝到生活的滋味。 | Sinful blood flows in our veins, a romanelle feels most alive when they are heartless and merciless, when they transgress the bounds of morality and justice. |
| 2:11 | 阿尔伯特 | 再多的财富也无法填满这个姓氏本性的空洞——优渥的生活没有让阿比盖尔变得不同，她孩子的外表在很长时间里掩盖了这一点。 | There is no fortune great enough to suppress such a lineage' as wicked obsession, not even for Abigail. |
| 2:21 | 阿尔伯特 | 我较为年长的两个孩子和我别无二致。 | And I suppose I needn't remind you of my other two children. |
| 2:28 | 阿尔伯特 | 劳伦斯在海外服役，好在殖民地胡作非为。布兰奇渴望美国的土地，因为那里有她在英国找不到的刺激。 | Lawrence served overseas also. He could indulge his dirty urges in the colonies. Blanche yearned for America, craving the excitement of a new world that home cannot offer her. |
| 2:42 | 阿尔伯特 | 而阿比盖尔……她不该生活在一个只有犯罪的世界里，成为那个残忍的雷蒙内尔。我在妻子的葬礼上下定决心让雷蒙内尔获得新生。 | But perhaps it is not too late for my dear abbe, she is not meant to inherit the curse of the romanel name. So at my wife's funeral, I made a silent vow to her that we would all turn over a new leaf. |
| 3:03 | 阿尔伯特 | 阿比盖尔是唯一的希望……我要找一条更好的路。 | For my daughter is the only hope I have for this cursed blood line. I resolved to find a new and better path for her. |
| 3:14 | 玛利亚 | …… | — |
| 3:18 | 根特督察 | 很抱歉让你在这种环境中等待这么久，玛利亚小姐。 | Sorry to have kept me waiting, miss. |
| 3:21 | 玛利亚 | 配合警方的调查是我的义务，根特督察，在这里我并没有感到什么不适。 | It's quite all right, sir. I am more than happy to do whatever may be necessary to serve the investigation. |
| 3:28 | 根特督察 | 嗯……如果你还有精神的话，就请来我的办公室聊一聊吧，伊芙琳也在那里等你。 | In that case, could I trouble you for another chat? Miss Evelyn is awaiting you in my office. |
| 3:35 | 伊芙琳 | 啊，玛利亚小姐，你来了。让我们快些结束这个不愉快的夜晚吧。 | Oh miss herb, you're here. Let's get this dreary evening over with, shall we? |
| 3:42 | 伊芙琳 | ——第一个问题，雷蒙内尔家的家庭教师是怎么和运河工人互助会扯上关系的？ | Question number one how did little miss romanel's governess get involved with the regent's canal friendly society? |
| 3:49 | 玛利亚 | 运河工人互助会是阿尔伯特先生生前资助的公益事业，他每个月会拿出一笔固定的款项，给在运河边工作的工人提供帮助。 | It' is a group that helps canal labourers in need. Financially supported by Mr romanel. Though not publicly. |
| 3:57 | 玛利亚 | 我算是他的……某种代理人，我会负责管理每个月提供给运河工人的现金。 | I act as his agent in some capacity. My duty is to distribute the monthly funds among the canal laborers who need them most. |
| 4:07 | 伊芙琳 | 他为什么这么做？什么时候开始的？你又为什么是他的代理人？——等等等等，我一次性问太多问题了。 | When did he start giving money out to this group? For what purpose? And why are you, of all people, doing the job on his behalf? Oh, sorry, I shouldn't have asked questions two, three and four all at once. |
| 4:17 | 玛利亚 | 没关系，我可以一个个回答。 | That's quite all right. I shall answer each one of them in turn. |

| 4:40 | 玛利亚 | 他没有把自己这么做的理由说得十分明白，不过我感受到他处在一种长期的焦躁状态中。 | As for his reasons for the donations, I'm afraid he never explained them to me. But I felt a sense that it was in part due to some form of torment that had long since hung over him. |
| 4:55 | 玛利亚 | 至于挑选我成为代理人的理由其实很简单，像我几个小时前和你说过的那样，我初来伦敦时穷困潦倒，是互助会的工人帮助了我。 | And as I shared with you earlier, the. Those labourers offered me tremendous help upon my arrival In London. I suppose that must be the reason Mr romane chose me to represent him. |
| 5:07 | 玛利亚 | 也是经他们的介绍，我才知道阿尔伯特先生家的情况——他有一个年幼的女儿正需要家庭教师的照顾。 | In fact, it was through them that I first learned that he was looking for a governess for his daughter. |
| 5:13 | 玛利亚 | 我读过一些书，也懂一些外语和社交礼仪，于是想试试这份工作。 | I received a decent education as a child and I have some knowledge of foreign languages and etiquette, so I decided to give the job a try. |
| 5:24 | 伊芙琳 | 你说过你是通过韦斯塔韦中介所才认识阿尔伯特的。 | I thought you said you met em through westaway's registry office. |

| 6:00 | 伊芙琳 | 我总得有些收获才行，对吧？奇怪的是，目前的证词里多次提到阿尔伯特是个守财奴，他怎么会突然开始热心于公益事业？ | My meticulous investigating had to turn up something useful, wouldn't you say? What got me curious was this why would a regular scrooge like him all of a sudden become a bleed not do gooder? |

| 4:34 | 阿比盖尔 | 那还得多久呀！ | But that'll take forever. |

| 4:49 | 玛利亚 | ……我不会。 | Of course not. |

| 5:37 | 阿比盖尔 | 关在笼子里的海怪可凶了，它带刺的触手差一点就要刮破我的鼻子。要是我能让它听话，它肯定能一下子就杀死所有猎物。 | The monster's tentacles stretched all the way out of its cage. One nearly smacked me on the nose. If I could tame such a beast, I'd be able to kill any kind of prey. |

| 6:40 | 劳伦斯 | 只是……我现在还没有想好该编一个怎样的故事，也许你和布兰奇会更擅长一些。 | But how does one begin to tell a child such a thing? Perhaps you and blanche can construct a suitable tale. |
| 6:48 | 玛利亚 | 她是一个坚强的孩子，我认为她理应得知真相。 | Abby is a strong girl. I believe she deserves to know the truth. |

| 6:59 | 劳伦斯 | 我听说，昨天警方展开的抓捕行动一无所获，嫌犯在警察撒下的天罗地网下逃之夭夭了。 | I heard the prime suspect managed to slip through the fingers of the entire police force yesterday. |
| 7:05 | 玛利亚 | …… | 啊？ |

| 7:21 | 劳伦斯 | 我认为他的说法很对，这两天我和律师在整理父亲的账簿，核对财产。一些繁琐的事，你不需要知道这些。 | I tend to agree with him. We have been accounting for father's assets these past few days, and their ledgers are nigh unto numerous to count. I shall not trouble you with the tedious details. |
| 7:33 | 劳伦斯 | 令我好奇的是，我发现父亲有一项支出，虽然数额不大，但每月都定期从银行支取，用途不明。 | But there was a monthly expense which particularly caught my attention. It wasn't a significantly large sum, but its purpose was rather unclear. |
| 7:44 | 劳伦斯 | 经过调查后，我有了一些发现。玛利亚，你知道运河工人互助会的事吗？ | After some investigation, I discovered something, miss. Are you familiar with the regent's canal friendly society? |
| 7:53 | 玛利亚 | 是的，我知道。 | ✓ Yes, sir, I am. |

| 8:07 | 劳伦斯 | 我明白其中的缘由，玛利亚，我知道父亲是怎么看待我和布兰奇的。 | Say no more, miss herb. I know father never thought highly of blanche and me. |
| 8:13 | 劳伦斯 | 你能在这个时候以诚实的态度对待我，证明我对你的看法也是正确的——你是一个值得信任的人。 | But your honesty proves my assessment of you correct. You are indeed worthy of trust. |
| 8:19 | 劳伦斯 | 只要你愿意，你仍然可以留在这里，我可以和你签新的合同，并且保证你会得到更高的薪酬。 | You shall remain under the employ of this house for as long as you wish. A new contract will also be drafted ensuring you higher pay. |
| 8:29 | 玛利亚 | 谢谢，不过阿尔伯特先生给我的薪资已经远超一般的市价，我也没有理由要求获得更高的报酬。 | That is very kind of you indeed, sir, but I do not believe I have done any deeds to deserve a higher salary. Mr romane's payment was already more than generous. |
| 8:41 | 劳伦斯 | 请不要推辞，我知道你对阿比盖尔来说有多重要。 | I won't take no for an answer. You and I both know how important you are to my little sister. |

| 9:53 | 玛利亚 | 他们无法生活，通常会在某条巷子里死去，或者……走上犯罪的道路，劳伦斯先生。 | They didn't, sir. They either perished in the cold or tends to criminality. |
| 9:59 | 劳伦斯 | …… | — |
